HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000318/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_13b3b609055de9fbbadc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:26:4\",\"root_000318\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesap, hatırlatma, insan denetiminin reddi, ceza ve dönüşten sonra ilahi tarafın açıkça üstlendiği son sayım ve sorumlu yönetim işlemidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1e40368852256e631b68","text":"This last ayah reorganized the entire surah as a chain of notice, response, return, and divine accounting. It also made earlier negations of sufficiency (`لا يغني`) and control (`لست ... بمصيطر`) resolve: sufficiency and supervision are finally divine.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000318/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1f79f3b840ab4b028d5f","text":"1. **Reckoning is the terminal operation explicitly assumed by the divine side.** **Lexical:** `ح س ب` B001, \"counting and reckoning,\" and B006, \"supervision and accountable management.\" **Cue:** delayed `ثُمَّ`, emphatic `إِنَّ`, and fronted `عَلَيْنَا` follow reminder, denial of human control, rejection, punishment, and return. **Role:** B001 supplies the audit, B006 supplies responsible administration, and `علينا` assigns that responsibility. **Abduction:** I infer a terminal procedural stage from `ثم` and sequence; I do not infer the detailed chronology of punishment versus account. **Reading change:** `حسابهم` is not an abstract total but accountable management whose owner is expressly named.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_201cabeb5e2db27fe455","text":"1. **Final reckoning as counted account.** Evidence: `ح س ب` B001 counting/reckoning; prior `ع م ل` B001 work, `س ع ي` B002 endeavor, `ذ ك ر` B009 reminder. Structural cue: the last ayah follows return and closes the surah. Role: reckoning evaluates the deeds and responses activated throughout. Abductive move: work, effort, refusal, and reminder require a counting endpoint. Reading change: the ayah reads as the final accounting mechanism of the whole packet.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B003","root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_21597b98f21bd26cef5d","text":"2. **Sufficiency relocated to God.** Evidence: `ح س ب` B003 sufficiency/enoughness; earlier `غ ن ي` B002 availing/sufficing is negated, `ل ي س` B001 negation, and `ن ع م` B001 favor. Structural cue: the punished food does not suffice, but the final account is “upon Us.” Role: divine reckoning is sufficient where creaturely provision failed. Abductive move: the last word can answer the earlier question of what avails. Reading change: `حساب` reads not only as arithmetic judgment but as adequate divine settlement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2457aa4981b9063a9d1e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:26:4\",\"root_000318\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesabın yeterlilik anlamı, ilahi hesabın tek başına yettiğini ve hatırlatanın buna zorlayıcı bir denetim eklemesinin meşru bir işlev taşımadığını bildirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3492cf1813345c7a52bb","text":"No later ayah exists in the packet, so no later-ayah activation or correction emerged. The retrospective reread instead confirmed that 88:21-25 consistently reserve reminder for the human role and leave supervisory reckoning anchored in `ح س ب` B001/B006 here.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4292a7af0bde1b30aeb1","text":"2. **Actual account replaces human supposition.** **Lexical:** `ح س ب` B002, \"reckoning as supposition,\" activated against B001 exact counting. **Cue:** 88:21 limits the messenger to reminder and 88:22 denies control, leaving no warrant for human estimation to become final judgment. **Role:** B002 names provisional mental assessment; B001 names the conclusive operation claimed in 88:26. **Abduction:** I infer correction from role limitation and final assignment. **Reading change:** the ayah distinguishes what people may think or infer from the definitive reckoning that is not theirs to administer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_880f800ae17667e40c73","text":"3. **Supervisory management without messenger domination.** Evidence: `ح س ب` B006 supervision/accountable management; `س طر` B003 control denied to the messenger; `ء ل ه` B002 divine name in 88:24. Structural cue: the final claim follows the explicit denial of human control. Role: account is a divine supervisory function, not a human one. Abductive move: the surah distinguishes reminder from management and management from coercive human oversight. Reading change: the final ayah seals the agency map: remind, do not control; they return; We account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9e84afcac3b481e3eb02","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:26:4\",\"root_000318\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Soyluluk ve sayılan değerler çağrışımı, görünür makam yerine yüz çevirme ve inkâr gibi eylemlerin kişinin değerini belirleyen hesaba girdiğini düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_000318/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9ec39085aca75053940a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:26:4\",\"root_000318\",[\"B002\",\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesabın sanı ve tahmini de çağrıştırması, insanların geçici değerlendirmesini kesin sayımdan ayırır; hatırlatan kişinin yorumu nihai yargıya dönüşemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ac1e1ca92ca708757f9b","text":"4. **The counted grounds are actions, not visible rank.** **Lexical:** `ح س ب` B004, \"nobility and counted merits,\" behind the account branch. **Cue:** the only conduct singled out in the closing window is turning away and rejecting in 88:23, while the messenger's status does not grant control in 88:22. **Role:** B004 raises the question of what counts toward standing; the sequence answers with accountable conduct rather than social office. **Abduction:** I infer that actions become the relevant counted grounds. **Reading change:** `حسابهم` evaluates the basis of worth itself, stripping visible authority or affiliation of any power to replace reckoned action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B004","root_001630/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ae35cb14555f4159677c","text":"4. **Honor counted or lost.** Evidence: `ح س ب` B004 nobility/countable merits; `و ج ه` B006 status/eminence, `ك ب ر` B005 rank, `ن ع م` B001 favor. Structural cue: faces are either humbled or easeful, and final counting decides standing. Role: account counts not just acts but the merits or standing manifested by faces. Abductive move: public face-status is grounded in what can be counted before God. Reading change: `حسابهم` includes the reckoning of honor and worth, not mere numerical tally.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d2addcf5cbb139c61b0a","text":"3. **Sufficiency closes the authority structure.** **Lexical:** `ح س ب` B003, \"sufficiency and enoughness,\" coactivated with B001/B006. **Cue:** the messenger's task is restricted to reminding, control is negated, and return plus account are assigned elsewhere. **Role:** B003 makes the claimed reckoning sufficient, requiring no supplementary coercion by the reminder. **Abduction:** I infer institutional sufficiency from the clean distribution of roles. **Reading change:** `علينا حسابهم` also says, functionally, that the divine account is enough; human overreach would add nothing legitimate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The paired labor scenes change حساب from a gross activity count into an audit of what effort was for and what it yielded.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"broader_than_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_ca9a755c6ec7e4282dac","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
